Hier ruht in Gott

Johann Ch. Mehling

dessen Gattin
Maria Barb. Mehling

Maria Otter
geb. Stamm

---------------------------------------------end-----DIV60--

===Date: 1995
===Photos: 2
file://./Images_1995/Mehling,Otter.jpg
file://./Images_2018/Grabstein%20Otter.jpg
===Notes:
Photo taken of this stone in 1995 when it had already been removed from the Friedhof.
Located outside of Otter family tractor barn,  Gertraudenweg, Waldzell.
2018-07-23: Monika Otter said this was on the "Förster's Grab", where the current stone for Ferdinand and Agnes is erected.
(It was called Förster's Grab even though Martin was not a Förster.)

pre 2018 info RJO never saw this marker in the Waldzell friedhof, but
it was said that it was in the Friedhof at one time and then removed. Presumably in this plot before 1995.
photo credit: Grabstein Otter.jpg taken by Monika Otter & Bernd Oehring on 2018-10-06.
